# W0-03E-A2C — active backend tenant access inventory

> GENERATED by `backend/scripts/w0_03e_a2c_inventory.py`. Do not edit by hand.
> Regenerate after any change to the data-access surface.

На човешки: таблицата по-долу изброява всяко място в активния backend, което чете или пише данни на фирма. Колоната `UNSCOPED` трябва да е празна — там са местата, където при еднакви ID-та данни могат да прескочат между две фирми.

## 1. Scope

The inventory covers the **whole active backend**, derived from the filesystem rather than a hand-written list: every `app/**/*.py`, `server.py`, and the operator scripts declared in the guard.

* modules inventoried: **198**
* tenant-owned accesses found: **2017** (1479 reads, 538 writes)
* static guard on the same scope: **0 violation(s)**

## 2. Classification summary

| class | accesses | reads | writes | meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TENANT_VIEW` | 1855 | 1384 | 471 | none — predicate applied by the access layer |
| `RAW_SCOPED` | 101 | 52 | 49 | low — predicate present but hand-written |
| `TENANT_DATABASE` | 22 | 13 | 9 | none — handle is this tenant's own database |
| `BOUNDARY_CODE` | 7 | 6 | 1 | n/a — this code IS the boundary |
| `PRE_TENANT` | 32 | 24 | 8 | declared — no tenant exists yet, or read-only report |
| `UNSCOPED` | 0 | 0 | 0 | CROSS-TENANT READ/WRITE when ids collide |

**`UNSCOPED` is empty: there is no active tenant-owned read or write without a server-resolved tenant predicate.** Issue #41 §8 requires active bare-ID reads = 0 and active bare-ID writes = 0.

Every tier is narrow, technically justified, and enforced by the guard; none contains tenant-owned business access.

## 4. Fixed-`_id` settings collision

`_id` is unique per collection, so a global literal `_id` means the installation can hold exactly one such row: the first tenant to save owns it and every other tenant's upsert fails with a duplicate key. `app.tenancy.settings_identity` gives each row the id `"<key>:<org_id>"`.

| setting key | legacy `_id` | A2C `_id` | readers / writers |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `worker_rates` | `worker_rates` | `worker_rates:<org_id>` | `app/routes/extra_works.py`, `app/routes/labor_smr.py`, `app/tenancy/settings_identity.py` |
| `employee_cost_config` | `employee_cost_config` | `employee_cost_config:<org_id>` | `app/routes/full_cost.py`, `app/tenancy/settings_identity.py` |
| `overtime_config` | `overtime_config` | `overtime_config:<org_id>` | `app/routes/work_sessions.py`, `app/tenancy/settings_identity.py` |

Migration: `backend/scripts/w0_03e_a2c_settings_identity_migration.py` (dry run by default, idempotent, resumable, never overwrites a differing tenant row, refuses an ownerless row).

## 6. Collection classification

Every collection the application names is classified in `app/tenancy/ownership.py`; an unclassified one fails the guard (`A2C-UNCLASSIFIED`) rather than passing.

* `ORG_KEYED` (tenant-owned, `org_id`): **124**
* `TENANT_ID_KEYED` (W0-03/W0-04, `tenant_id`): **3** + prefixes ['md_']
* `TENANT_ROOT`: `organizations`
* `TECHNICAL`: **1**
